# 听力

> 按年份和题号整理；文字、选项和答案完全相同的题只保留一份，并在标题列出原卷标注。

## 2013年

### 第1题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

听下面5段对话，每段对话有一道小题，从每题所给的A、B、C三个选项中选出最佳选项。
What room does the man want?
A. Single
B. Double
C. Twin
答案：A
### 第1题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What is the man's job?
A. A basketball player.
B. A laundry worker.
C. A window washer.
D. A rock climber
答案：C
解析：这是一道事实细节题。从对话中可知，男士清洗办公楼的窗户。所以选项为C，他是个窗户清洁工。关键词句：I wash office building windows；to reach the window.
### 第1题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

How many sisters does the woman have?
A. One.
B. Two.
C. Three.
### 第1题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

What does the man want to do?
A. Take photos.
B. Buy a camera.
C. Help the woman.
### 第1题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

What does the man want to do?
A. Take photos.
B. Buy a camera.
C. Help the woman.
答案：A
### 第1题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

Man: I've got my camera with me. Am I allowed to take photos here? Woman: I think so. It doesn't say you can't. What does the woman mean?
A. The man can take photos.
B. Photos are not allowed.
C. The camera is not permitted.
答案：A
### 第1题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

Why doesn't John go to school today?
A. It's the weekend.
B. He's too tired for school.
C. His school is closed down.
答案：A
### 第1题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

Where is the man going?
A. Kennedy.
B. London.
C. New York.
### 第2题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

What will the man buy?
A. Vegetables
B. Meat
C. Bread
答案：C
### 第2题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the woman imply?
A. She is not hungry.
B. She wants to cook.
C. She is not tired.
D. She wants to dine out.
答案：D
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，女士说太累了一点儿也不想做饭，可推测她想出去吃。所以答案是D。dine out 表示出去吃饭。关键词：too tired to do any cooking. 注意too…to表示"太……而不能……"。
### 第2题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What is the woman going to do tonight?
A. Meet the man.
B. Go out for dinner.
C. Prepare for a meeting.

What are the speakers talking about?

B. Go jogging
C. Go camping
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the woman think of her hometown?
A. Promising
B. Isolated
C. Crowded
D. Modern
答案：B
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，女士认为她的家乡美丽而幽静。但却与世隔绝。所以答案是B，isolated 表示隔绝的，孤立的。关键词句：But it's really far away from everything. be far away from表示"远离"。听时注意"but"这个转折词后的信息。
### 第3题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

Who has a dictionary?
A. The man.
B. Lucy.
C. Tina.
### 第3题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年江西卷）

Where is the man now?
A. On his way.
B. In a restaurant.
C. At home.
### 第3题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

Where is the man now?
A. On his way.
B. In a restaurant.
C. At home.
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

A: Hi, Maggie. I'm coming, but it's snowing and the traffic is moving slowly. B: Ok, David. Take your time. We'll wait for you, so we can have dinner together. Why is David delayed?
A. The heavy snow and slow traffic.
B. He has to wait for Maggie.
C. He is preparing dinner.
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

What is the male speaker?
A. A student.
B. A president.
C. A professor.
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

What is the woman doing?
A. Taking a taxi.
B. Doing shopping.
C. Waiting for the man.
### 第3题（原卷：2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

Where is the man now?

B. In a restaurant.
C. At home
### 第4题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

How much is the change?
A. $8
B. $42
C. $50
答案：B
### 第4题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

Where will the speakers probably go?
A. To a stationery shop.
B. To a gymnasium.
C. To a paint store.
D. To a news stand.
答案：A
解析：这是一道场景题。从对话可知，女士说打印机里面没纸了，她跑去买纸，男士说一起去。所以答案是A，stationery shop 是文具店，符合题意。B是体育馆，C是油漆店，D是报摊。关键词句：My printer is out of paper. I will run and get some.
### 第4题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What are the two speakers most probably?
A. Students.
B. Workers.
C. Teachers.
### 第4题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

What will Celia do?
A. Find a player.
B. Watch a game.
C. Play basketball.
### 第4题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

What will Celia do?
A. Find a player.
B. Watch a game.
C. Play basketball.
答案：C
### 第4题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

A: Celia, you see those girls over there? They need another player for a basketball game. Would you like to join them? B: Seems like it's a game for fun. Sure, I'll be there in a minute. What will Celia do?
A. Watch the game.
B. Go shopping.
C. Join the basketball game.
答案：C
### 第4题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

What does the man mean?
A. The door is unlocked.
B. He is not the one to blame.
C. Somebody has just left the lab.
答案：B
### 第4题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

Where are the two speakers going to meet?
A. At home.
B. In a shop.
C. In a restaurant.
### 第5题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the woman mean?
A. The man can see a different view.
B. The food is not tasty enough.
C. The man cannot afford the food.
D. The food is worth the price.
答案：D
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，男士认为菜单上的价格太高，女士说你尝了菜之后就会改变观点，言下之意是你吃了之后就不觉得贵。所以答案是D，The food is worth the price.这里的食物物有所值。关键词：have a different view, delicious food.
### 第5题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What are the two speakers talking about?
A. A language.
B. An interest.
C. A class.
### 第5题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

What day is it when the conversation takes place?
A. Saturday.
B. Sunday.
C. Monday.
答案：B
### 第5题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

A: I won't have anything to wear to work on Monday unless I pick up my clothes at the dry cleaner's. B: Then you'd better hurry. It closes at noon on Sundays. When did the man probably go to the dry cleaner's?
A. On Saturday.
B. On Sunday morning.
C. On Sunday afternoon.
答案：B
### 第5题（原卷：2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

What day is it when the conversation takes place?

When will the woman go to see the movie?
A. Friday
B. Saturday
C. Sunday
答案：B
### 第6题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the woman imply?
A. She reads different kinds of books.
B. She also finds the book difficult to read.
C. She is impressed by the characters.
D. She knows well how to remember names.
答案：B
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，男士说读完这本书很困难。女士说她懂他的感觉，谁能记住35个不同的人物角色的名字。言下之意是她也不能记住，觉得太难了。所以答案是B。关键词句：Who could remember…？听时注意说话者的反问语气。

Which city is the woman going to?
A. Chicago.
B. Boston.
C. New York.

What is Sara going to do?
A. Buy John a gift.
B. Give John a surprise.
C. Invite John to France.
答案：B

A: Alright, Sara, we know that you are planning something big for John's birthday. What has Sara planned?
A. To send John's sister a plane ticket.
B. To hold a party at home.
C. To buy John a gift from France.
答案：B
### 第6题（原卷：2013年江西卷）

What is Sara going to do?

B. Give John a surprise.

### 第7题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What can we learn from the conversation?
A. The man will go to the post office.
B. The post office is closed for the day.
C. The woman is expecting the newspaper.
D. The delivery boy has been dismissed.
答案：C
解析：这是一道主旨题。从对话可知，女士疑惑他们没收到报纸。男士说有时邮寄需要花点时间。言下之意是女士在期盼报纸的到来。所以答案是C。关键词：is expecting, 表示"期望，等待"。

### 第8题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What can we learn about the woman?
A. She is not sure if she can join them.
B. She will skip the class to see the film.
C. She will ask the professor for leave.
D. She does not want to see a film.
答案：A
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，女士想去看电影，但是她不确定7:30是否老师会准时下课，因为老师通常拖堂。而男士和他室友7:40就要出发。所以答案是A。关键词句：class ends at 7:30；the professor never finishes on time. 老师从来不准时下课。

### 第9题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the man mean?

B. School learning is a must for fashion designers.

D. The woman should become a fashion designer.
答案：B
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，男士的建议是设计师要找个好学校好好学习。所以答案是B, 学校教育是时装设计师必经之路。关键词句：Go to school；Find a good school and learn as much as you can.

What does Diana want Peter to do?
A. Help her with her study.
B. Take a book to her friend.

What will Peter do this weekend?
A. Go to Hong Kong with Dan.

答案：B

Where is the man expected to go tomorrow?
A. The agency.

答案：B

Who can handle the summarizing?

B. Tom.
C. Mary.

听第8段材料，回答第10至12题。

答案：B
### 第10题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the man mean?
A. Few people drive within the speed limit.
B. Drivers usually obey traffic rules.
C. The speed limit is really reasonable.
D. The police stop most drivers for speeding
答案：A
解析：这是一道推理题。从对话可知，女士要求男士减速，男士却说大多数驾驶者通常都会忽视车子限速，除非他们觉得警察会拦下他们。所以答案是A, 很少有人会驾驶不超速。关键词句：Most drivers usually ignore the speed limit.
### 第10题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What does the woman advise the man to do?
A. Join a reading club.
B. Travel to other countries.
C. Collect stamps.

答案：B

Why does the man call the woman?

C. To plan a meeting with her.
答案：A

A: Hello, this is Andrea. B: Hello, Andrea, this is Alex. Miranda was very satisfied with you. How does Andrea feel about the news?

C. Worried.

### 第10题（原卷：2013年江西卷）

Why does the man call the woman?

### 第11题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

听下面一段独白，回答第11至13题。
What was the speaker's previous job?
A. A book publisher.
B. A company manager.
C. A magazine editor.
D. A school principal.
答案：D
解析：说话人以前的工作是什么？图书publisher是短文提及但与问题无关，这是说话人创办公司之后的事；公司经理、杂志编辑都是目前的工作。正确答案为学校校长，说话人在一开始就明确表明 My previous job was as principal of a language school。
### 第11题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What are the two speakers mainly talking about?
A. Stamps.
B. Hobbies.
C. Books.
### 第11题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

Who needs a new flat?
A. Alex.
B. Andrea.
C. Miranda.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

When can Andrea start her new job?
A. On Monday.
B. On Wednesday.
C. On Friday.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

Who needs a new flat?
A. Alex.
B. Andrea.
C. Miranda.
### 第11题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

What is the greatest advantage of the woman's flat?
A. Its size.
B. Its condition.
C. Its location.
答案：C
### 第11题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

Which does the woman need to bring?
A. A map.
B. A tent.
C. Some clothes.
### 第12题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

Where is the man planning to go?
A. Boston
B. Washington D.C.
C. New York
答案：C
### 第12题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What helps to make the speaker productive according to the passage?
A. Some training experience.
B. A happy family.
C. Russian assistants' help.
D. A good memory.
答案：D
解析：根据短文信息，说话人工作效率高的原因是什么？培训经验只说I received awards for training teachers to teach more effectively；幸福家庭只是介绍了家庭情况；俄罗斯助手的帮助these visitors helped a lot at home and made life easier，均与工作效率无直接联系。正确答案为好的记忆力：I am grateful I have a photographic memory.... Otherwise, I might not be that productive.
### 第12题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What is the woman going to do on Friday?
A. Attend a meeting.
B. Go on vacation.
C. Make a phone call.
### 第12题（原卷：2013年江苏卷）

Where is the woman now?
A. In Baltimore.
B. In New York.
C. In Avon.
答案：B
### 第12题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

Where is the woman now?
A. In Baltimore.
B. In New York.
C. In Avon.
答案：A
### 第12题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

Why can't Andrea start on Monday?
A. She has to visit her father and find a flat.
B. She has to move to Avon.
C. She needs to buy furniture in New York.
答案：A
### 第12题（原卷：2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

Where is the woman now?
A. In Baltimore.
B. In New York.
C. In Avon.
### 第12题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

Who will the woman consult later?
A. Her agent.
B. Her husband.
C. Her neighbour.
答案：B
### 第12题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

Who will prepare the meals?
A. The man.
B. The guide.
C. The woman.
### 第13题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

听第9段材料，回答第13至15题。
Where does the speaker get information for the trip?
A. The internet
B. Travel agents
C. Guidebooks
答案：A
### 第13题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the passage mainly tell us?
A. Lynn's devotion to the family.
B. Lynn's busy and successful life.
C. Lynn's great performance at work.
D. Lynn's efficiency in conducting programs.
答案：B
解析：短文主要讲述什么？Lynn对家庭的付出、在工作上的成就、运行项目的高效都属于以偏概全。正确答案为Lynn忙碌又成功的生活——短文前半部分讲述说话人在事业上的成功，后半部分讲述说话人在忙碌中平衡工作和家庭。
### 第13题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

Who is most probably going to meet the man at the airport?
A. The woman.
B. The man's brother.
C. The woman's sister.
### 第13题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年江西卷）

What does Jan consider most important when he judges a restaurant?
A. Where the restaurant is.
B. Whether the prices are low.
C. How well the food is prepared.
### 第13题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

What does Jan consider most important when he judges a restaurant?
A. Where the restaurant is.
B. Whether the prices are low.
C. How well the food is prepared.
答案：C

W: Hello, Mr. Jan Erick Freedman. You're a frequent traveler. How come you're so fond of eating out? What is Jan Erick Freedman?
A. A traveler and food lover.
B. A hotel clerk.
C. A taxi driver.
答案：C
### 第13题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

Who is Alexander?
A. The woman's boss.
B. The woman's host.
C. The woman's cook.
答案：A
### 第13题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

What are the two speakers doing?
A. Discussing a lecture.
B. Visiting a museum.
C. Attending a party.
### 第13题（原卷：2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

听第9段材料，回答第13至16题。
What does Jan consider most important when he judges a restaurant?
A. Where the restaurant is.
B. Whether the prices are low.
C. How well the food is prepared.
### 第14题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

What public transportation does the speaker seldom take?
A. Planes
B. Buses
C. Trains
答案：B
### 第14题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

听下面一段独白，回答第14至16题。
What kind of questions are usually asked in a traditional interview?
A. Economic questions.
B. Routine questions.
C. Academic questions.
D. Challenging questions.
答案：B
解析：传统型面试经常会问什么类型的问题？经济类问题、学术性问题短文未提及；具有挑战性的问题是case interview所问问题的特点。正确答案为常规性问题：This is usually just a series of standard questions…标准问题实质上就是常规性问题，答案是对原文的同义转换。
### 第14题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

What is the possible relationship between the two speakers?
A. Parent and teacher.
B. Brother and sister.
C. Husband and wife.
### 第14题（原卷：2013年江苏卷、2013年江西卷、2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

When did Jan begin to write for a magazine?
A. After he came back to Sweden.
B. Before he went to the United States.
C. As soon as he got his first job in 1982.
### 第14题（原卷：2013年安徽卷、2013年辽宁卷、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷）、2013年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

When did Jan begin to write for a magazine?
A. After he came back to Sweden.
B. Before he went to the United States.
C. As soon as he got his first job in 1982.
答案：A
### 第14题（原卷：2013年福建卷）

How did Jan start collecting favorite restaurants?
A. People asked him for recommendations.
B. He worked for a magazine.
C. He ran a restaurant.
答案：A
### 第14题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

What extra dish is to be prepared?
A. Fish.
B. Steak.
C. Shrimps.
答案：B
### 第14题（原卷：2013年湖南卷）

Which job has Jane applied for?
A. Salesgirl.
B. Waitress.
C. Volunteer.
### 第15题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

What kind of hotels does the speaker usually stay in?
A. Hotels with low prices
B. Hotels providing meals
C. Hotels near sightseeing places
答案：A
### 第15题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the case interview focus on about the candidate?
A. Work experience.
B. Educational qualifications.
C. Problem-solving abilities.
D. Information-gathering abilities.
答案：C
解析：案例面试关注应试者的哪些方面？工作经验、教育资历都是traditional interview关注的问题；收集信息的能力短文未提及。正确答案为解决问题的能力：…a series of questions to find out how the candidate would approach the problem.案例面试的目的就是考查求职者解决问题的能力。
### 第15题（原卷：2013年重庆卷）

At what time are they leaving for the Natural Museum?
A. 7:30 a.m.
B. 8:30 a.m.
C. 3:30 p.m.

What may Jan do to find a good restaurant?

### 第16题（原卷：2013年北京卷）

听下面一段对话，完成第16至20题，每小题仅填写一个词。
Customer Pick-up/Delivery Form（取件/配送表）

### 第16题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What does the speaker mainly talk about?
A. Features of different types of interview.
B. Skills in asking interview questions.
C. Changes in three interview models.
D. Suggestions for different job interviews.
答案：A
解析：说话人主要谈论的是什么？问面试问题的技巧、三种面试模式的变化、对不同面试的建议短文均未提及。正确答案为不同类型面试的特征：短文开头就点明了主旨，Job interviews can generally be divided into three types.

What will they possibly do right before supper?
A. Swim at the beach.

C. See an exhibition.

What do we know about Jan?

### 第16题（原卷：2013年湖北卷）

答案：Street
### 第17题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

听下面一段对话，完成第17至20题。Complete the form. Write ONE WORD for each answer.
Latest Conference Information
Date: 8th __17__
Place: Palace __18__, Shanghai
Registration fee: $ __19__
Speaker: Carla Marisco from Milan University
Speech topic: Opportunities and Risks in the __20__ Market
答案：November
解析：这是一道事实细节题。从对话中可知，女士正打电话向男士确认关于会议修改的事宜。从女士所说的I'm calling about the conference in Shanghai on November 8th.可知本题答案为November。

What do we know about the Plaza Leon?

答案：African
解析：这是一道事实细节题。从对话中可知，女士告知男士说米兰大学虽然换了发言人，但是发言的主题并没有改变，从对话中的Opportunities and Risks in the African Market可知，本题答案为African。

Jack Brown 的职业（Occupation）为？

Why does the speaker like Horatio Street best?

### 第20题（原卷：2013年高考英语（全国大纲卷））

Why does the speaker like Horatio Street best?

C. It is a famous university.
### 第21题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

听下面一段对话，完成第21至24题。Complete the form. Write NO MORE THAN THREE WORDS for each answer.
An Interview with David, a Skateboarding (滑板运动) Lover
What was David's schoolwork like?
He was able to get his schoolwork done __21__.
What was his only problem at school?
He was unable to __22__ in class.
Why did he say the new headmaster was wonderful?
He let students __23__ of their own.
How was his new style different from other skaters?
It was robot-like, with __24__.
答案：with good grades
解析：这是一道事实细节题。当采访人询问David的成绩时，David回答说That was fine. I was able to get my school work done with good grades.所以本题答案为with good grades。
### 第22题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

What was his only problem at school?
He was unable to __22__ in class.
答案：sit still
解析：这是一道事实细节题。David热爱滑板，成绩也不错，但是唯一的问题是他的精力太旺盛了，以至于不能安安静静坐在教室中。从David的话语My only problem was that I had so much physical energy that I could not sit still in class.可知本题答案为sit still。
### 第23题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

Why did he say the new headmaster was wonderful?
He let students __23__ of their own.
答案：plan/create PE classes
解析：这是一道事实细节题。David在聊到自己因为热爱滑板而转学时，对新学校的校长表示了称赞，从The headmaster there was wonderful. He let us plan our own P.E. classes.可知本题答案为plan P.E. classes。
### 第24题（原卷：2013年上海卷（秋考））

How was his new style different from other skaters?
It was robot-like, with __24__.
答案：new tricks
解析：这是一道事实细节题。David在提到自己和其他滑板运动者的区别时，他说... but I was kind of like a robot always coming up with new tricks.可知本题答案为new tricks。

What will Lucy do at 11:30 tomorrow?
A. Go out for lunch.
B. See her dentist.
C. Visit a friend.
答案：B
解析：简单的事实细节题。根据材料中女士的话"but I have an appointment with my dentist at 11:30."可知女士明天上午11:30要去看牙医。

D. On August 8th.
答案：B
### 第2题（原卷：2016年江苏卷、2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What is the woman going to do?
A. Help the man.
B. Take a bus.
C. Get a camera.
答案：B
### 第2题（原卷：2016年浙江卷（10月））

What is the time now?
A. 8 o'clock.
B. 10 o'clock.
C. 12 o'clock.
答案：A
### 第2题（原卷：2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

What is the weather like now?
A. It's sunny.
B. It's rainy.
C. It's cloudy.
答案：C
解析：简单的事实细节题。根据材料中女士的问话"Is it still raining?"及男士的回答"No, but there's still lots of clouds."可知现在虽然雨停了，但是有很多乌云。
### 第3题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

Who is most probably the man?
A. A waiter.
B. A butcher.
C. A porter.
D. A farmer.
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2016年江苏卷、2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What does the woman suggest the man do?
A. Tell Kate to stop.
B. Call Kate's friends.
C. Stay away from Kate.
答案：A
### 第3题（原卷：2016年浙江卷（10月））

What are the speakers talking about?
A. A local artist.
B. The man's salary.
C. An apartment to let.
答案：C
### 第3题（原卷：2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

Why does the man talk to Dr. Simpson?
A. To make an apology.
B. To ask for help.
C. To discuss his studies.
答案：A
解析：意图推断题。根据材料中男士的话"I'm sorry I was late for class today, Dr. Simpson."及"I won't let it happen again."可知男士迟到了在跟他的老师道歉。
### 第4题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

How will the woman get back from the railway station?
A. By train.
B. By car.
C. By bus.
答案：C
解析：简单的事实细节题。根据材料中女士的话"I'm coming back this afternoon, and I'll take a bus from the railway station."。

What does Jenny decide to do first?

C. Get an assistant.
答案：B

C. Meet with Professor Smith.
答案：C

B. Colleagues.

### 第8题（原卷：2016年浙江卷（10月））

听第7段材料，回答第8至10题。

### 第8题（原卷：2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2016年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第7段材料，回答第8至10题。
What are the speakers talking about?
A. Having guests this weekend.
B. Going out for sightseeing.
C. Moving into a new house.
答案：A
解析：主旨大意题。根据材料可知双方谈话的主旨是Pamela和Peter本周末要来做客。对话的第一句话"I've just received an email from Pamela. She and Peter are coming down to see us this weekend."点明了主旨。

What is the relationship between the speakers?
A. Neighbors.
B. Husband and wife.
C. Host and visitor.
答案：B
解析：人物关系的推断题。根据材料中男士的话"I can't wait to show them around our new house."和女士的话"We haven't had any guests since we moved in here."可知他们是夫妻关系。

Where probably is the speaker?
A. In a park.
B. In a hotel.
C. In a shopping centre.
答案：B
解析：地点推断题。根据材料中"You'll find a copy of your weekend's schedule in your room."和"Go and leave your luggage in your rooms. I'll be seeing you here again in fifteen minutes."可知导游在安排旅客安顿行李，所以地点可能是在酒店。
### 第21题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

Blanks 21 through 24 are based on the following conversation. Complete the form. Write NO MORE THAN THREE WORDS for each answer.
Who is Sue Walter? She is __21__ in court and a writer.
答案：a (famous) judge
### 第22题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

What is Sue's suggestion for people with difficulties? __22__
答案：Asking for help
### 第23题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

In Sue's eyes, what is the best part about her job? __23__ in decision-making.
答案：Having a voice
### 第24题（原卷：2016年上海卷（秋考））

What does Sue think happiness is? __24__
答案：Sharing (with others)

答案：A
### 第1题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听下面5段对话。每段对话后有一个小题，从题中所给的A、B、C三个选项中选出最佳选项。

M: How was it?
W: Jason thought it was extremely amusing, but I was a bit disappointed.

### 第6题（原卷：2017年江苏卷）

听第6段材料，回答第6、7题。
What is the woman looking for?
A. An information office.
B. A police station.
C. A shoe repair shop.
答案：C
### 第6题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

听第6段材料，回答第6、7题。
M: Lily, don't you think the scholarship from New York University is something great? Why did you decline this offer?
W: I do realize the value of the scholarship. But, if I could explain, my mother doesn't want me to go overseas.
M: Why?
W: You know, I'm the only child in the family. My mother will be living alone most of the time for three years in my absence. She cannot even think of it.
M: Nobody to take care of her?
W: My father travels a lot to many countries and he is doing business in Dubai now. I have to do a lot for my mom at home.
M: Oh, I see. You are very considerate.
Why does the man sound surprised?
A. Lily rejected a job offer.
B. Lily was absent from school.
C. Lily turned down a scholarship.
答案：C
### 第6题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听第6段材料，回答第6、7题。
W: Excuse me. I wonder if you could tell me how to find a place to have my shoes mended. I'm new in town.
M: Ah, there is a good shop not far from here. Go straight ahead and walk about three blocks. I can't remember the name of the shop, but you'll find it. It's near the police station. By the way, you know about the Town Guide? It's a thin book and has all kinds of useful information. You'll find one in any bookstore.
W: Thanks a lot! You've been so helpful. Let's see. Did you say the repair shop was three blocks away from here?
M: Exactly.
W: Thanks again.
What is the woman looking for?
A. An information office.
B. A police station.
C. A shoe repair shop.
答案：C
### 第6题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第6段材料，回答第6、7题。
M: Mum, this is going to take forever. Animal World will be on in twenty minutes. It's my favorite program. You know I can't miss it.
W: I know, honey, but we have to wait in line to pay for these things we need.
M: You promise we'd be home in time.
W: Be a little patient, Jack. We'll get out of here soon.
What does Jack want to do?
A. Watch TV.
B. Play outside.
C. Go to the zoo.
答案：A
### 第7题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What is the Town Guide according to the man?
A. A brochure.

What has Lily decided to do?
A. Travel to Dubai.
B. Stay with her mom.
C. Start a business.

Where does the conversation probably take place?
A. At home.
B. In a cinema.
C. In a supermarket.

### 第8题（原卷：2017年江苏卷）

听第7段材料，回答第8、9题。
What does the man say about the restaurant?
A. It's the biggest one around.
B. It offers many tasty dishes.
C. It's famous for its seafood.
答案：B
### 第8题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

听第7段材料，回答第8至10题。
M: Anna just emailed to say that the managers' meeting is put off till next Monday. Will you have everything ready by then, Sabrina? Hey, Sabrina, what's wrong?
W: I'm so worried. I haven't heard from my sister for two weeks.
M: How often do you call each other?
W: Normally at least once a week, but she's now a volunteer teacher at a mountain village in Africa. I can only write her.
M: The mail can be really slow sometimes. I'm sure you'll hear from her soon.
W: I hope so.
M: You know the saying, "no news is good news." If something were wrong, someone would have called you.
W: Maybe you're right. Thanks, Jason.
What is the probable relationship between the speakers?
A. Colleagues.
B. Relatives.
C. Classmates.
答案：A
### 第8题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听第7段材料，回答第8、9题。
M: I've been here many times. There are quite a lot of delicious dishes to choose from. What are you thinking of ordering?
W: Well, I haven't decided yet. What are you going to order?
M: I think I'll have the roast chicken. They really make it well here.
W: I had roast chicken yesterday when I ate out with Shelly.
M: Their beef steak is good, too. You can have it served with beans and mushrooms.
W: But I'm not that hungry. Is the fried fish or the seafood salad good?
M: Never had them before. Maybe if you get the steak, we could share.
W: That sounds like a good idea.
What does the man say about the restaurant?
A. It's the biggest one around.
B. It offers many tasty dishes.
C. It's famous for its seafood.
答案：B
### 第8题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第7段材料，回答第8至10题。
M: Hi, Samantha, do you have a minute?
W: Hi, Richard, come in, please.
M: I've been trying to get hold of you all afternoon.
W: I was at the weekly managers' meeting. How were things?
M: Oh, splendid. I have some news.
W: Good news?
M: Wonderful news, to me, anyway. I've been offered a job for three years as an assistant to Professor Hayes from Birmingham University. I'll join him in Mexico City. What do you think about that?
W: Wonderful, Richard. Just what you've always wanted, isn't it? Doesn't mean that you'll leave soon?
M: Next week. But before I go, I'd like to invite you out for dinner. Just thank you for all the help you gave me during my three months research work here. Would tomorrow evening be a good time for you? We can go to the Shanghai Restaurant in Chinatown.
W: Fine with me.
What does Richard do?
A. He's a newsman.
B. He's a manager.
C. He's a researcher.
答案：C
### 第9题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What will the woman probably order?
A. Fried fish.
B. Roast chicken.
C. Beef steak.
答案：C
### 第9题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

What is Sabrina's sister doing?
A. Touring in Africa.
B. Teaching in a village.
C. Working in a company.
答案：B
### 第9题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

Where is Richard going next week?
A. Birmingham.
B. Mexico City.
C. Shanghai.
答案：B
### 第10题（原卷：2017年江苏卷）

听第8段材料，回答第10至12题。
Where will Mr. White be at 11 o'clock?
A. At the office.
B. At the airport.
C. At the restaurant.
答案：B
### 第10题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

How can Sabrina reach her sister now?
A. By phone.
B. By email.
C. By letter.
答案：C
### 第10题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听第8段材料，回答第10至12题。
W: Hello, Mr. White. Do you think it's possible for us to talk sometime today?
M: I'd love to, Miss Wilson, but I've got a pretty tight schedule today. I've got to finish reading the yearly financial report by 10. Then I have to drive to the airport to pick up an advertiser at 11. After that, I'll have a meeting with him over lunch.
W: Can I see you after lunch?
M: Well, let me see… after lunch, I have to attend a senior staff meeting, which may last about two hours. Can you come at 3? We can talk for an hour before I meet my sales team at 4.
W: I'm afraid an hour is too short. What about tomorrow morning?
M: 9 to 11, then. I'll wait for you at the office.
W: OK, see you then.
Where will Mr. White be at 11 o'clock?
A. At the office.
B. At the airport.
C. At the restaurant.
答案：B
### 第10题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

What will the speakers do tomorrow?
A. Eat out together.
B. Visit a university.
C. See Professor Hayes.
答案：A
### 第11题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What will Mr. White probably do at one in the afternoon?
A. Receive a guest.
B. Have a meeting.
C. Read a report.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

听第8段材料，回答第11至13题。
W: I'm not sure about this soup, Carl. It has no taste.
M: No! I don't think so, Maria. It tastes fine to me. We've been to this place before, and I don't feel the soup tastes any different.
W: Well, I still think it needs something. Salt?
M: No, certainly not. What about pepper? If anything, I think it could use a little more of that.
W: Now you're talking. That's exactly what it needs. And how about some more onions too?
M: I don't know about that. You seem to be starting to like food with strong tastes recently.
W: Oh, really?
What does Maria think of the soup?
A. Tasteless.
B. Just fine.
C. Thick.
答案：A
### 第11题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第8段材料，回答第11至13题。
M: What's that book you just picked up, Mary?
W: The one Professor Lee uses in his course.
M: Oh, I see. You'd better have it if you want to pass that course.
W: But it costs $30. I simply can't afford it.
M: Did you check the used-book section here? Maybe they have it.
W: No, they don't. I asked.
M: Why don't you get it from the library?
W: I've been trying for a month and it's always out. There are over 50 students in the course, and every single one wants the book.
M: Listen, you know my roommate, Henry, don't you? He took the same course last year. And I remember he owns a copy. I can borrow it from him for you.
W: Oh, that would be great. Thank you, Jim.
What is the probable relationship between the speakers?
A. School friends.
B. Teacher and student.
C. Librarian and library user.
答案：A
### 第12题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

When will Miss Wilson see Mr. White?
A. At lunch time.
B. Late in the afternoon.
C. The next morning.
答案：C
### 第12题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

What does Karl say can be added to the soup?
A. Salt.
B. Onions.
C. Pepper.
答案：C
### 第12题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

Why does Jim suggest Mary buy the book?
A. It's sold at a discount price.
B. It's important for her study.
C. It's written by Professor Lee.
答案：B
### 第13题（原卷：2017年江苏卷）

听第9段材料，回答第13至16题。
Why is Bill going to Germany?
A. To work on a project.
B. To study German.
C. To start a new company.
答案：A
### 第13题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

Where are the speakers?
A. At home.
B. At a restaurant.
C. At a friend's house.
答案：B
### 第13题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听第9段材料，回答第13至16题。
W: Hey, Bill. I hear they're going to send you to Germany for the new project.
M: Boy, news travels fast around here! I only got the orders a couple of hours ago. Rachel doesn't even know…
W: Oh, no? So, what are you waiting for? When do you plan to tell her?
M: Well, she is at work at the moment. She doesn't finish until 5. I'll have to wait until tonight now, I suppose.
W: Well, I was posted there before, back in 2008. It's OK — a bit like England, really. At least the weather is similar, and the people aren't much different. The only thing I didn't like is the food, especially the sausages. What do you intend to do about the family? Are you going to take them with you?
M: Well, I'd like to, but I don't know much about the situation at the moment… you know, about schools and all that. But I hope to move the family out there in a couple of months' time. I don't think I want to spend a year and a half out there on my own… I mean, without Rachel and the kids. I mean, I don't see that much of them now as it is.
W: Yeah. Well, that's the way it is normally in our company, I suppose.
M: Yeah. Well, we'll see. Let me pay for the coffee.
W: No, no. I'll pay.
Why is Bill going to Germany?
A. To work on a project.
B. To study German.
C. To start a new company.
答案：A
### 第13题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

What will Jim do for Mary?
A. Share his book with her.
B. Lend her some money.
C. Ask Henry for help.
答案：C
### 第14题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What did the woman dislike about Germany?
A. The weather.
B. The food.
C. The schools.
答案：B
### 第14题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

听第9段材料，回答第14至16题。
M: Good morning, I'm one of the students who rented your flat. It's 55 Park Road.
W: Oh, yes. Everything all right?
M: Not exactly. I'm afraid there are a couple of problems.
W: Oh! I'm sorry to hear that. What kind of problems?
M: Well, we haven't had any hot water for a couple of days now. I wonder if you could send someone to have a look at it.
W: Of course. I'll get someone to come around at the weekend.
M: Well, could he come around a bit sooner? I don't think we can manage until the weekend.
W: I see. Okay. I'll send someone over this afternoon then.
M: There's also the matter of the fridge. We all assumed there would be one in the flat when we moved in, because that's what we read from the advertisement in the newspaper.
W: Ah, yes. Sorry about that. I got rid of the old fridge, but I didn't get around to ordering a new one yet. I'm really sorry. I'll order one today and get it delivered to you tomorrow.
M: We bought one on the Internet actually. But could you pay us back?
W: Of course. Just tell me how much you paid for it.
M: It's 260 pounds. Thank you.
When will someone come to check the hot water?
A. This afternoon.
B. Tomorrow.
C. At the weekend.
答案：A
### 第14题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第9段材料，回答第14至16题。
W: Hello.
M: Hello, Stella. This is Peter.
W: Hi, Peter. Fancy hearing your voice. How are you?
M: Couldn't be better. I'm planning a trip to Memphis with my family this summer.
W: Great, I'd love to host you here. When are you coming?
M: We'll leave Boston on June 20th. Stay in St Louis Missouri for a week, and then fly to your city. We'd probably stay for five days with you and come back. For it's a two-week vacation.
W: Is there anything special you'd like to do here?
M: We'd love to explore Beale Street, the official home of the blues, where we'd listen to live music. We'd also like to visit Graceland, the popular museum in memory of Elvis Presley. But above all, we want to spend some time with you and your family.
W: Of course, it's been ages since we met last time. Let's have a dinner party in my garden.
M: Lovely. Oh, could you find for us a hotel near where you live? It doesn't have to be big, but I can't stand noisy at bedtime.
W: Sure.
M: Thank you. See you soon.
Where does Stella live?
A. In Memphis.
B. In Boston.
C. In St Louis.
答案：A
### 第15题（原卷：2017年江苏卷、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

What does Bill hope to do about his family?

W: Where are you sending your package?

### 第17题（原卷：2017年江苏卷）

听第10段材料，回答第17至20题。
When did it rain last time in Juárez?
A. Three days ago.
B. A month ago.
C. A year ago.
答案：C
### 第17题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

听第10段材料，回答第17至20题。
M: Hello everyone! I'm very happy to be invited to this program today. I'd like to share with you my impression of Paris. Actually, there's something interesting to see in every corner of Paris: the old buildings, neighborhoods, and the historical monuments. The weather's been great too, cool and dry. Some aspects of life here are different from those in the United States. The most obvious thing is that there is much more activity in the streets than in a typical US city. People gather together in open markets, cafes, parks, and squares. Then, of course, there's the food. Food seems to be more important here. It's beautifully shown in windows and markets. Everything is fresher and better tasting than at home. I've gained five pounds since I arrived. There are a few things that bother me though. I'm still having a hard time with the language. People speak very fast and it's hard to get them to repeat things. People, in general, are less warm here and it's hard to make friends. I guess that can be true in any big city in a new country, but the French do seem to be less friendly than the people back home.
Who is the speaker?
A. An invited guest.
B. A news reporter.
C. A radio host.
答案：A
### 第17题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

听第10段材料，回答第17至20题。
W: Hello, everyone. Welcome to our program. I'm Jenny Jackson. The weather here in Juárez has become unbearable. With no rains for over a year, the city is suffering from unusually hot weather. Some light showers have been forecast since last month, but all of them have been effective in surrounding areas. Summer has not even started yet, but temperatures have reached 40 degrees centigrade in the past three days. And people have been warned not to go out of their homes — not if you walk on foot, at least — between 11 in the morning and 7 in the evening. Little kids and the elderly are the ones who have suffered the most from this extreme heat wave. They must drink water all the time. To help our audience have a better understanding of this extreme weather, we have invited Prof. Torres from University of Mexico to our studio this evening.
When did it rain last time in Juárez?
A. Three days ago.
B. A month ago.
C. A year ago.
答案：C
### 第17题（原卷：2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2017年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

听第10段材料，回答第17至20题。
Hello everyone. My name is John. I'm the teaching assistant for this lab session. Let me explain a little about it. As you may know already, it's required meeting once a week. I expect you to do all the experiments and keep the results in your lab notebook. I'll collect the notebooks every two weeks. You'll be graded on lab notebooks and quizzes. But the most important information I want to give you now is about safety. First of all, you must wear shoes that cover your feet in the lab. Tennis shoes are OK; also don't wear loose clothes, long scarves, big necklaces or loose belts. They get cut in something or fall into liquid. Another thing to do for safety is cleaning up.
Be sure to put the waste in the correct containers. We can't mix liquid with paper. This is extremely important. I don't want any fires in this room. You are responsible for washing out your own lab equipment and putting it away. If you don't do this, I will take away points from your grade. Nobody is going to clean up after you. OK, any questions?
How many lab sessions will the students have every week?
A. One.
B. Two.
C. Three.
答案：A
### 第18题（原卷：2017年北京卷）

W: When is it convenient for the mailman to fetch the package?
M: At 5 o'clock, Monday afternoon.
Q: When will the mailman fetch the package?
答案：Monday/Mon.

What season is it in Juárez?
A. Spring.
B. Summer.
C. Autumn.
答案：A
### 第18题（原卷：2017年浙江卷（11月））

A. He speaks faster.

答案：C

B. Classmates.
C. Strangers.
答案：C

B. She is looking for the man.

### 第6题（原卷：2019年浙江卷（6月）、2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

W: Last month.

How long did James run his business?
A. 10 years.
B. 13 years.
C. 15 years.
答案：C

M: Yes, of course, couldn't find it. Well, just get a new one then.
What does the man want the woman to do?
A. Check the cupboard.
B. Clean the balcony.
C. Buy an umbrella.
答案：C

What has Kate's mother decided to do?
A. Return to school.
B. Change her job.
C. Retire from work.
答案：A
### 第8题（原卷：2019年浙江卷（6月）、2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

W: Guess what? My mother's decided to go back to school.
M: Why?
W: Well, she always loved art, but learned business administration at college, because her parents thought it was difficult for an artist to find a job.
M: So she wants to study art now?
W: Yeah, oil painting. It's been her dream for a long time.
What has Kate's mother decided to do?
A. Return to school.
B. Change her job.
C. Retire from work.
答案：A
### 第8题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

M: Hi, Lucy. How was your weekend?
W: Great! You know, we went mountain climbing in the forest park. The air was so different from the city, cool and fresh.
M: Sounds great!
W: Yeah, there wasn't a cloud in the sky and we could see for miles from the top. What about you? What did you do?
M: Busy packing.
W: Oh, yes, of course! I forgot you and Jenny are moving into a new house.
Where did the woman go at the weekend?
A. The city centre.
B. The forest park.
C. The man's home.
答案：B

What is the man doing?
A. Chairing a meeting.

C. Conducting a job interview.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2019年浙江卷（6月））

M: Dear listeners, for today's show, I have with me, my colleague, Mary Lenny. She has been a radio and TV reporter for many years. Mary, welcome to our show!
W: It's a pleasure to be here.
What is the man doing?
A. Chairing a meeting.
B. Hosting a radio program.
C. Conducting a job interview.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

M: Dear listeners, for today's show, I have with me, my colleague, Mary Lenny. She has been a radio and TV reporter for many years. Mary, welcome to our show!
W: It's a pleasure to be here.
M: Would you please tell our listeners who most influenced your decision to become a reporter?
W: Both my parents had a great influence on my choice of work.
M: How did they do that?
W: My father always told me that an education was one of the greatest advantages I could have. He used to tell me that readers were leaders, and encouraged me to read all I could. As a result, I've always kept up with the newspapers, faithfully read magazines and learned to really enjoy books, all of which have been a valuable help to me in radio and television reporting.
M: What about your mother?
W: Well, my mother helped me in a much different way.
What is the man doing?
A. Chairing a meeting.
B. Hosting a radio program.
C. Conducting a job interview.
答案：B
### 第11题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

M: Hello, can I speak to Mr. Stone, please?
W: Hello, but I'm afraid Mr. Stone is in a meeting until lunchtime. Can I take a message?
M: Well, yes. I'd like to make an appointment to see him. It's Harrison White here.
W: Just a second, Mr. White. I'll look in the diary.
What is Mr. Stone doing now?
A. Eating lunch.
B. Having a meeting.
C. Writing a diary.
答案：B

（Questions 14 through 16 基于关于messiness与creativity的短文，本解析卷仅保留选项。）

### 第14题（原卷：2019年江苏卷）

Why does the man seldom do exercise?
A. He lacks motivation.
B. He has a heart problem.
C. He works all the time.
答案：A
### 第14题（原卷：2019年浙江卷（6月）、2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

M: We all know that exercise is good for us, but sometimes it seems too hard to leave the sofa.
W: I can see that. You seldom do exercise.
M: Plus, having the doctor tell us to get two and a half hours' exercise a week doesn't really help our motivation much.
W: Don't be discouraged. Now a new study suggests getting benefits from exercise doesn't have to be that demanding. Jacob Sattelmair from Harvard University has done a study into how much exercise is needed to lower the risk of heart attacks.
Why does the man seldom do exercise?
A. He lacks motivation.
B. He has a heart problem.
C. He works all the time.
答案：A

### 第17题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷））

M: Hello, everybody. Today I'd like to share with you something about John Audubon. John was born on April 26th, 1785. He spent much of his childhood in France where he spent many hours each day outdoors painting pictures. At the age of 18, he moved to the United States. Well, actually, John began his life in the US as a businessman. However, instead of taking care of his business, John was often off exploring nature. At first, John spent his time hunting for fun. Then he found that the animals around were very beautiful, and he particularly enjoyed observing the birds he saw. Therefore, he painted the birds he observed a lot. We are lucky now to have so many paintings of American birds by John Audubon.
What did John enjoy doing in his childhood?
A. Touring France.
B. Playing outdoors.
C. Painting pictures.
答案：C
### 第17题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））

M: Hello, everybody. Today I'd like to share with you something about John Audubon. John was born on April 26th, 1785. He spent much of his childhood in France where he spent many hours each day outdoors painting pictures. At the age of 18, he moved to the United States. John began his life in the US as a businessman. However, instead of taking care of his business, John was often off exploring nature. At first, John spent his time hunting for fun. Then he found that the animals around were very beautiful, and he particularly enjoyed observing the birds he saw. We are lucky now to have so many paintings of American birds by John Audubon.
What did John enjoy doing in his childhood?
A. Touring France.
B. Playing outdoors.
C. Painting pictures.
答案：C
### 第18题（原卷：2019年上海卷（春考））

A. In men's brains, there are stronger connections in two sides of the brain.
B. In men's brains, there are stronger connections in each half of the brain.
C. The connections in men's brain are not so strong as those in women's brain.
D. There is nothing different between male and female brain.
### 第18题（原卷：2019年江苏卷）

What did the scientists do to the road?
A. They repaired it.
B. They painted it.
C. They blocked it.
答案：B
### 第18题（原卷：2019年浙江卷（6月））

Here is a piece of news for bird lovers. Scientists have painted a long road, red, yellow and white. They help to discourage the sea birds from wandering onto the highway. Young birds are often attracted to the warm roads' surface and get killed by the traffic. Biologist Christen says the plan is to see how the birds respond to the multi-colored road this summer.
What did the scientists do to the road?
A. They repaired it.
B. They painted it.
C. They blocked it.
答案：B
### 第18题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅰ卷））

Here is a piece of news for bird lovers. Scientists have painted a long road, red, yellow and white. They help to discourage the sea birds from wandering onto the highway. The area is home to large crowds of birds that come to stay for the season. Young birds are often attracted to the warm road's surface and get killed by the traffic. Biologist Christen says the plan is to see how the birds respond to the multi-colored road this summer.
What did the scientists do to the road?
A. They repaired it.
B. They painted it.
C. They blocked it.
答案：B
### 第18题（原卷：2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅱ卷）、2019年高考英语（新课标Ⅲ卷））
